The 4 agents run concurrently and each one's completion arrives as a message in this conversation when it finishes — do NOT issue a separate blocking call to wait for them.\n285\t\n286\t**Once all 4 agents have reported completion, read each agent's output file (single message with 4 Read calls):**\n287\t```\n288\tRead tool:\n289\t  file_path: \"{outputFile from that agent's async_launched result}\"\n290\t```\n291\t\n292\t> Allow up to `workflow.subagent_timeout` for the slowest agent to finish before treating it as failed. The timeout is configurable via `workflow.subagent_timeout` in `.planning/config.json` (milliseconds). Default: 300000 (5 minutes). Increase for large codebases or slower models.\n293\t\n294\tEach output file contains that agent's completion confirmation. Parse the confirmation marker (see below) from the file contents.\n295\t\n296\t**Expected confirmation format from each agent:**\n297\t```\n298\t## Mapping Complete\n299\t\n300\t**Focus:** {focus}\n301\t**Documents written:**\n302\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC1}.md` ({N} lines)\n303\t- `.planning/codebase/{DOC2}.md` ({N} lines)\n304\t\n305\tReady for orchestrator summary.\n306\t```\n307\t\n308\t**What you receive:** Just file paths and line counts. NOT document contents.\n309\t\n310\tIf any agent failed, note the failure and continue with successful documents.\n311\t\n312\tContinue to verify_output.\n313\t</step>\n314\t\n315\t<step name=\"sequential_mapping\" condition=\"Agent tool is NOT available (e.g. Antigravity, Codex)\">\n316\tWhen the `Agent` tool is unavailable, perform codebase mapping sequentially in the current context. This replaces `spawn_agents` and `collect_confirmations`.\n317\t\n318\t**IMPORTANT:** Do NOT use `browser_subagent`, `Explore`, or any browser-based tool. It runs on\n376\tevery mapping run, incremental (`--paths`) and full alike. `--files` narrows it\n377\tto the documents an Update run actually refreshed; `--paths` needs no narrowing\n378\tbecause all seven are regenerated, just scoped in content.\n379\t\n380\t**Why this is a shell step and not an instruction to the mapper.** The stamp is\n381\tthe only machine-readable freshness marker: the `verify codebase-drift` gate\n382\treads it to decide what to diff HEAD against. The human-readable markers the\n383\tmapper writes (`**Analysis Date:**`, `<!-- refreshed: ... -->`) are restamped\n384\tunconditionally on an Update run, so a mapper that decides its work is already\n385\tdone and rewrites only the dates still looks fresh to a human. Leaving the\n386\tmachine-readable stamp to the same agent reproduces exactly the failure the\n387\tstamp exists to detect. A shell step cannot be skipped by a confident agent.\n388\t\n389\tThe command is non-blocking: it emits `skipped` with a `reason` outside a git\n390\trepo or when no documents exist. Report `stamped` and `commit` in the summary\n391\tif any entry in `failed` is non-empty; otherwise continue silently.\n392\t\n393\tRun in this position, before `commit_codebase_map`, the stamp lands on\n394\tdocuments the mapper just wrote, so its markdown whitespace normalization is\n395\tfolded into the same commit. Running `stamp-codebase-map` by hand against an\n396\talready-committed map reflows that map's whitespace as a side effect.\n397\t\n398\tContinue to scan_for_secrets.\n399\t</step>\n400\t\n401\t<step name=\"scan_for_secrets\">\n402\t**CRITICAL SECURITY CHECK:** Scan output files for accidentally leaked secrets before committing.\n403\t\n404\tRun secret pattern detection:\n405\t\n406\t```bash\n407\t# Check for common API key patterns in generated docs\n408\tgrep -E '(sk-[a-zA-Z0-9]{20,}|sk_live_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|sk_test_[a-zA-Z0-9]+|ghp_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|gho_[a-zA-Z0-9]{36}|glpat-[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+|AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}|xox[baprs]-[a-zA-Z0-9-]+|[REDACTED:gsd-scan]|eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.eyJ[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+\\.)' .planning/codebase/*.md 2>/dev/null && SECRETS_FOUND=true || SECRETS_FOUND=false\n```\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=true:**\n\n```\n⚠️  SECURITY ALERT: Potential secrets detected in codebase documents!\n\nFound patterns that look like API keys or tokens in:\n[show grep output]\n\nThis would expose credentials if committed.\n\n**Action required:**\n1. Review the flagged content above\n2. If these are real secrets, they must be removed before committing\n3. Consider adding sensitive files to Claude Code \"Deny\" permissions\n\nPausing before commit. Reply \"safe to proceed\" if the flagged content is not actually sensitive, or edit the files first.\n```\n\nWait for user confirmation before continuing to commit_codebase_map.\n\n**If SECRETS_FOUND=false:**\n\nContinue to commit_codebase_map.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"commit_codebase_map\">\nCommit the codebase map:\n\n```bash\ngsd_run query commit \"docs: map existing codebase\" --files .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\nContinue to offer_next.\n</step>\n\n<step name=\"offer_next\">\nPresent completion summary and next steps.\n\n**Get line counts:**\n```bash\nwc -l .planning/codebase/*.md\n```\n\n**Output format:**\n\n```\nCodebase mapping complete.\n\nCreated .planning/codebase/:\n- STACK.md ([N] lines) - That is acceptable for a portfolio, but it is the marginal price of the \"both libraries\" decision, so use react-spring where it earns it.\n91\t- Full `motion` component is the heavy part (about 53 kB with presence/spring/gesture features). motion.dev's own figures: `motion` about 34 kB, `m` + `LazyMotion` about 4.6 kB initial (+15 kB `domAnimation`, +25 kB `domMax`), `useAnimate` mini about 2.3 kB. **Optional optimization (not tested):** because motion-primitives is copy-pasted source, you could swap `motion.x` for `m.x` and wrap the app in `LazyMotion`. `AnimatePresence`/`layout`/`drag` need `domMax`, which erodes the saving. That belongs in an early performance spike.\n94\t\n95\t## The Four Named Tools\n96\t\n97\t### 1. motion-primitives (ibelick) : USE, as a copy-in source library. Confidence: MEDIUM-HIGH\n98\t\n99\t- Only deps are `motion`, `clsx`, `tailwind-merge`, `lucide-react`, plus a `@/lib/utils` `cn()` helper. **No shadcn init required.**\n100\t- Components are served as shadcn-registry JSON at `https://motion-primitives.com/c/<name>.json` (HTTP 200 verified).\n101\t- The official CLI is `npx motion-primitives@latest add <name>`. It works (tested) but the npm package is stale (0.1.0, March 2025) and **drops files into `./components/motion-primitives/` at the project root, not `src/`**. Either move them to `src/components/motion-primitives/` or skip the CLI and copy the registry JSON content. Decide once and document it.\n102\t- Needs the `@` alias in both `vite.config.ts` (`resolve.alias`) and `tsconfig.app.json` (`paths: { \"@/*\": [\"./src/*\"] }`). With TS 6/7, `paths` works **without** `baseUrl` (verified in the probe). shadcn's Vite doc still says to add `baseUrl`. You do not need it.\n103\t\n104\t### 2. react-spring : USE, scoped. Confidence: HIGH\n105\t\n106\t- Install `@react-spring/web@~10.1.2`. Import `useSpring, useSprings, useTrail, useChain, useTransition, animated, config` from `@react-spring/web`.\n107\t- Version note: `react-spring` (the umbrella, 10.0.4) is behind and v11.0.0-beta (beta.1 released 2026-10-01) is **removing it**, going ESM-only, and deprecating `useTransition` in favor of new `usePresence`/`usePresenceList`. Stay on 10.1.x stable. Avoid `useTransition` for new core UI (use motion's `AnimatePresence`) so the v11 migration stays trivial.\n108\t- Reduced motion is built in: `useReducedMotion` is exported from `@react-spring/web` (confirmed in the type definitions), and the global flag `Globals.assign({ skipAnimation: true })` snaps every spring to its target. Wire it once (snippet below).\n109\t- Dependencies are tiny and tree-shakable. Measured at about +17 kB gz for `useSpring/useTrail/useSprings`.\n110\t\n111\t### 3. realtimecolors.com : USE as a design tool, with caveats. Confidence: HIGH\n112\t\n113\t- The seed (`050315 / fbfbfe / 2f27ce / dedcff / 433bff`, Inter/Inter) is Text, Background, Primary, Secondary, Accent. Note `050315` on `fbfbfe` is the **light** theme pairing (dark text on light bg). The tool's light/dark toggle (Alt+T) lets you design both modes in one session. The project requires light and dark, so design both explicitly rather than inverting.\n114\t- It shows AA/AAA contrast traffic lights per role. Use them as the accessibility gate for text and interactive colors.\n115\t- Exports: CSS variables, Tailwind CSS, SCSS, and custom templates, in HEX/RGB/HSL/OKLAB/OKLCH. The tool lists a `tailwind.config.js` style Tailwind export and does not mention v4. **Export \"CSS\" with OKLCH and paste into `@theme`**, not the Tailwind-config export:\n116\t- Its type-scale presets (1.067 to 1.618) map to a `--text-*` ladder in `@theme`.\n117\t- The user wants alternatives explored: do that in the design phase (Realtime Colors' scheme generators: analogous/complementary/triadic, plus randomize with Space). Keep exactly 5 role tokens plus derived tints so swapping palettes later is a single-block edit.\n118\t\n119\t### 4. godly.design/posts : USE as inspiration only, not a dependency. Confidence: MEDIUM\n120\t\n121\t- `godly.design/posts` is a curated **feed of linked social posts** (mostly X links to designers' UI interaction clips), with category nav (Websites, Tools, Templates, etc.). No dates, tags, RSS, or API were visible. Treat it as a mood-board source: capture screenshots/links into the planning docs, then extract motion patterns (spring feel, press states, cursor effects) into a written reference list before coding. (Probe also noted `godly.website` currently 301-redirects to `recent.design`, so the brand's structure may have shifted. Rely on `godly.design/posts/` and verify when you actually browse.)\n122\t- No code or package implications.\n123\t\n124\t## Division of Responsibility (motion vs react-spring)\n125\t\n126\t| Concern | Owner | Why |\n127\t|---------|-------|-----|\n128\t| Mount/unmount, `AnimatePresence`, exit animations | **motion** | Mature, handles React 19 strict mode and transitions (13.1.1 and 13.4.6 fixes). |\n129\t| Layout and shared-element transitions (`layout`, `layoutId`, Morphing Dialog) | **motion** | react-spring has no equivalent. |\n130\t| Scroll-linked values (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, ScrollProgress, in-view reveals) | **motion** | Already bundled. 13.4.x moved offsets onto hardware-accelerated `ViewTimeline` where supported. |\n131\t| Text/number effects, stagger reveals, all motion-primitives prefabs | **motion** | They are written against it. |\n132\t| Simple drag, `whileTap`, `whileHover` | **motion** | Built in. No extra gesture lib. |\n133\t| Velocity-carrying physics: flick/toss, drag-release with momentum, rubber-band, pointer-chasing follower, squish-on-press toys | **react-spring** | `useSpring` with continuous retargeting is its sweet spot and gives the \"tactile\" signature. |\n134\t| Staggered chains of springs (`useTrail`, `useChain`) for playful sets of elements | **react-spring** | Concise API. |\n135\t| Anything in `@react-spring/three` | **react-spring** | Only if 3D is adopted. |\n136\t\n137\t## Tailwind v4 Notes\n138\t\n139\t- v4 `hover:` only applies under `@media (hover: hover)`, so hover styles never stick after a tap on phones. This matches the \"no desktop-only gestures\" requirement. Do **not** restore v3 behavior with `@custom-variant hover (&:hover)`.\n140\t- Browser floor: Safari 16.4+, Chrome 111+, Firefox 128+ (uses `@property`, `color-mix()`). Fine for this audience.\n141\t- No `tailwind.config.js`; `tw-animate-css` (the shadcn replacement for tailwindcss-animate) is not needed since motion handles animation.\n142\t\n143\t## Dark Mode (covered: Tailwind v4 + tiny script)\n144\t\n145\t## Font Loading\n146\t\n147\t- `import '@fontsource-variable/inter/wght.css'` in `main.tsx`. Verified: it declares `font-display: swap`, `font-weight: 100 900`, and per-script `unicode-range`, so a browser downloads only the **latin subset (48 kB woff2)** for English copy (latin-ext is 85 kB and only fetched if used). Vite emits all subsets to `dist/` (harmless unused files).\n148\t- `@fontsource-variable/inter/opsz.css` adds optical sizing (latin 73 kB). Worth it only if you want display-size tuning.\n149\t- **Preload is not automatic**: a bare `<link rel=\"preload\" href=\"@fontsource-variable/inter/files/...\">` in `index.html` is *not* rewritten by Vite (verified, stays unresolved). If you want a preload, use a small `transformIndexHtml` plugin or `import url from '...woff2?url'`. Skipping preload is acceptable with `font-display: swap`; revisit if Lighthouse flags it.\n150\t- If the design adds a display face from Realtime Colors, use the same Fontsource route (one variable family, latin only), and keep to 2 families total for mobile performance.\n151\t\n152\t## GitHub Pages Deploy\n153\t\n154\t## Alternatives Considered\n155\t\n156\t| Category | Recommended | Alternative | Why Not |\n157\t|----------|-------------|-------------|---------|\n158\t| Framework | Vite + React | Next.js / Astro | Next explicitly out of scope. Astro would be lighter on JS but the user wants React islands everywhere and these two React animation libs. |\n159\t| Animation split | motion for UI choreography + react-spring for signature physics | react-spring only | Loses `layoutId`/`AnimatePresence`/scroll and every motion-primitives component. |\n160\t| Animation split | (same) | motion only | Viable and smaller (saves 17 kB), but the user explicitly wants to try react-spring. Revisit if the spike shows react-spring adds nothing. |\n161\t| react-spring package | `@react-spring/web` | `react-spring` umbrella | Lagging, `deprecated` tag present, removed in v11 beta. |\n162\t| Installing primitives | Copy via registry JSON / CLI, then patch | Wait for an npm package | None exists. Source ownership is the model. |\n163\t| Styling | Tailwind v4 | Tailwind v3.4 | Only for pre-2023 browsers. motion-primitives and shadcn are on v4. |\n164\t| TypeScript | 6.0.x | 7.0.x | 7.0.2 compiled the probe cleanly, so it is available, but v6 is the scaffold default. Take 7 post-launch. |\n165\t| Dark mode | Tailwind data-attr + inline script | next-themes | Next-oriented abstraction we do not need. |\n166\t| Fonts | Fontsource self-hosted | Google Fonts `<link>` | Third-party request, render-blocking, privacy. |\n167\t\n168\t## What NOT to Use\n169\t\n170\t| Do not use | Why |\n171\t|------------|-----|\n172\t| `react-spring` umbrella package | Deprecated tag, removed in v11. Use `@react-spring/web`. |\n173\t| `react-spring` v11 beta | Beta, breaking `onRest`/`immediate` semantics. Wait for stable. |\n174\t| Raw `framer-motion` imports | motion 14 depends on `framer-motion` internally. Always import from `motion/react` so there is one copy. |\n175\t| motion-primitives `Cursor`, `Magnetic`, `Tilt`, `Spotlight` as *primary* interactions | Mouse-only. They are desktop garnish. |\n176\t| `shadcn init` | Unneeded; adds Radix/CVA you will not use. |\n177\t| `tailwind.config.js`, `@tailwind base/components/utilities` | v3 patterns. |\n178\t| `@use-gesture/react` by default | Last published March 2024, and motion already provides drag/tap/hover. Only add if a react-spring-driven drag cannot be done well otherwise. |\n179\t| `vite-plugin-image-optimizer` as the primary image tool | Compress-only (no srcset/format variants). Last publish Oct 2025. |\n180\t| Hard-coded absolute asset paths | Break when `base` changes. |\n181\t| Both libraries animating the same element/property | The one real coexistence hazard. |\n182\t\n183\t## Gaps: capabilities the four tools do NOT cover\n184\t\n185\t| Gap | Already covered? | Candidate A (default) | Candidate B | Notes |\n186\t|-----|------------------|-----------------------|-------------|-------|\n187\t| **Scroll-driven animation** | **Yes, by `motion`** (`useScroll`, `useTransform`, `scroll()`, motion-primitives ScrollProgress/InView) | motion `useScroll` (+0 kB) | GSAP ScrollTrigger (+44 kB gz measured). Smooth scroll is a taste item, not a requirement. The site is public and has no login or user accounts.\n\n## Fonts\n\n- `@fontsource-variable/archivo` - self-hosted Archivo variable font, bundled by Vite from `node_modules`. No Google Fonts or other CDN request.\n\n## Monitoring & Observability\n\n**Error Tracking:**\n- None detected.\n\n**Analytics:**\n- None detected. No analytics script in `index.html` or `404.html`.\n\n**Logs:**\n- Not applicable for the static site. Build and test output goes to the CI console.\n\n## CI/CD & Deployment\n\n**Hosting:**\n- GitHub Pages, using the \"GitHub Actions\" source. Workflow: `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`.\n- Deploy job uses `actions/deploy-pages` (pinned by commit SHA) with `permissions: pages: write, id-token: write` and the `github-pages` environment.\n- After deploy, the workflow `curl`s the published page URL and greps for the site owner's name to confirm the deploy served content.\n\n**CI Pipeline:**\n- GitHub Actions.\n  - `.github/workflows/ci.yml` - on every `push` and `pull_request`: `npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `npx playwright install --with-deps chromium`, `npm run test:e2e`.\n  - `.github/workflows/deploy.yml` - on push to `main` and `workflow_dispatch`: `build` job (`npm ci`, `npm run verify`, `actions/configure-pages`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact` of `./dist`), then `e2e` job (`needs: build`), then `deploy` job (`needs: [build, e2e]`).\n- Actions are pinned to full commit SHAs with version comments.\n- `actions/checkout`, `actions/setup-node`, `actions/configure-pages`, `actions/upload-pages-artifact`, `actions/deploy-pages`.\n\n## Environment Configuration\n\n**Required env vars:**\n- None for runtime or for building locally with defaults.\n- `VITE_BASE` - optional; set in both workflows to `/aa-phan-portfolio-v2/` (the deploy workflow comments that the cutover sets it to `/`).\n\n**Secrets location:**\n- No application secrets. No `secrets.*` references in the workflows. Deploy uses the GitHub-provided OIDC token (`id-token: write`), not a stored secret.\n- Local `.env*` files may exist; they are not read or documented.\n\n## Webhooks & Callbacks\n\n**Incoming:**\n- None.\n\n**Outgoing:**\n- None.\n\n## Test-Time External Access\n\n- Playwright e2e tests run against a local build; global setup in `tests/e2e/global-setup.ts`.\n- `pdfjs-dist` is used by the test suite to inspect `public/resume.pdf` (the resume-related specs in `tests/e2e/resume.spec.ts`).\n\n---\n\n*Integration audit: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3885,"cache_read_input_tokens":57483,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3885,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01TQsrYKU4gwz4HKmE9ywYTJ","agent_id":"ab292f3acd27cf53f","session_id":"528fc7c7-ca76-4a8d-986b-657c219e4755","uuid":"635ac0ae-1629-47a3-a4d6-d9a35c416572","timestamp":"2026-11-20T05:42:39.119Z","request_id":"req_011ChDeRbvZ4JZKkmcNQQxcx","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and arch"}

refreshed: 2026-11-19 -->\n# Architecture\n\n**Analysis Date:** 2026-11-19\n\n## System Overview\n\n```text\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│                 Browser entry: index.html / 404.html              │\n│                        `src/main.tsx`                             │\n└──────────────────────────────┬───────────────────────────────────┘\n         ▼\n┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐\n│  Page (single route): `src/app/Page.tsx`                          │\n│  reads `siteContent` once, owns scroll-spy via useActiveSection   │\n├──────────────┬───────────────┬───────────────┬───────────────────┤\n│ SiteHeader / │  Sections     │  Section      │  Footer           │\n│ PillNav      │  `src/sections│ /motion-prim…` │\n└────────────────┘\n```\n\n## Pattern Overview\n\n**Overall:** Single-page, static, component-driven React app. Content is typed TypeScript data, rendered by presentational section components. There is no router, no server, and no client-side data fetching.\n\n**Key Characteristics:**\n- One route. `src/app/Page.tsx` composes every section in order; navigation is in-page scroll via `SECTION_IDS` from `src/layout/nav-items.ts`.\n- Content is data-first. `src/content/index.ts` exports a single `siteContent` object consumed only by `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n- Animation is isolated behind a barrel. Only `src/anim/index.ts` is the public surface for animation; vendored motion-primitives live in `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n- Theme is driven by the DOM. `html[data-theme]` is the single source of truth; `src/lib/theme.ts` reads, writes, and syncs it.\n- Build-time asset pipeline. Images are imported with vite-imagetools query strings in `src/content/images.ts`, so the bundle contains resolved `<picture>` sources.\n\n## Layers\n\n**App shell / composition:**\n- Purpose: Bootstraps React, wraps global providers, composes the one page.\n- Location: `src/main.tsx`, `src/app/`\n- Contains: `App.tsx` (wraps `Providers`), `Providers.tsx` (motion preference, lazy motion features, toast context), `Page.tsx` (composition and scroll-spy).\n- Depends on: `@/anim`, `@/components/ui/Toast`, `@/content`, `@/hooks/useActiveSection`, `@/layout/*`, `@/sections/*`.\n- Used by: `src/main.tsx` only.\n\n**Content (data):**\n- Purpose: All copy and structured data for the site.\n- Location: `src/content/`\n- Contains: `profile.ts`, `contacts.ts`, `experience.ts`, `projects.ts`, `skills.ts`, `education.ts`, `images.ts`, `schema.ts` (types), `index.ts` (barrel).\n- Depends on: `schema.ts`; `images.ts` depends on `@/assets/images/*` through imagetools.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx` only (enforced by lint, see Architectural Constraints).\n\n**Sections (feature UI):**\n- Purpose: One component per page section, receiving data as props.\n- Location: `src/sections/` - `Hero/`, `Experience/`, `Projects/`, `About/`, `Skills/`, `Education/`, `Contact/`.\n- Contains: section components plus local helpers (`Projects/ProjectDialog.tsx`, `Projects/Sleeve.tsx`, `Projects/project-types.ts`).\n- Depends on: `@/layout/Section`, `@/layout/Container`, `@/components/ui/*`, `@/anim`, `@/hooks/*`, `@/lib/*`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n\n**Layout (chrome):**\n- Purpose: Site header, navigation, footer, and shared wrappers.\n- Location: `src/layout/`\n- Contains: `SiteHeader.tsx`, `PillNav.tsx` (mobile pill nav), `Footer.tsx`, `Section.tsx`, `Container.tsx`, `SkipLink.tsx`, `Wordmark.tsx`, `nav-items.ts`.\n- Depends on: `@/anim`, `@/components/ui/ThemeToggle`, `@/layout/nav-items`.\n- Used by: `src/app/Page.tsx`, sections.\n\n**UI primitives:**\n- Purpose: Reusable interactive pieces and hooks.\n- Location: `src/components/ui/`\n- Contains: `CtaLink.tsx`, `ThemeToggle.tsx`, `Toast.tsx` (provider and hook), `useToast.ts`, `useCopyEmail.ts`, `icons/*`.\n- Depends on: `@/lib/copy`, `@/lib/theme`, `@/anim`.\n- Used by: sections and layout.\n\n**Motion layer:**\n- Purpose: All animation and reduced-motion handling.\n- Location: `src/anim/`\n- Contains: `index.ts` (barrel), `MotionPreferenceProvider.tsx`, `useMotionPreference.ts`, `MotionFeatures.tsx` (lazy `LazyMotion` boundary), `features.ts` (the code-split feature bundle), `featuresStatus.ts`, `tokens.ts` (springs, durations, easing), `declarative/` (`NavIndicator.tsx`, `PillMorph.tsx`, `ToastPresence.tsx`), `physics/ThemeThumb.tsx` (react-spring).\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@react-spring/web` (only inside this folder).\n- Used by: `src/app`, `src/layout`, `src/sections`, `src/components/ui` via the barrel only.\n\n**Vendored primitives:**\n- Purpose: Copy-in motion-primitives source, owned by this repo.\n- Location: `src/components/motion-primitives/` (for example `text-effect.tsx`).\n- Depends on: `motion/react`, `@/lib/utils`.\n- Used by: `src/sections/Hero` role-line effect via the restricted-import exception. Imports must not come from sections directly (see lint rules).\n\n**Hooks:**\n- Purpose: Cross-cutting browser state.\n- Location: `src/hooks/`\n- Contains: `useActiveSection.ts` (scroll-spy plus click lock for nav), `useTheme.ts` (wraps `src/lib/theme.ts` subscribe and apply).\n\n**Lib (pure helpers):**\n- Purpose: Framework-agnostic utilities.\n- Location: `src/lib/`\n- Contains: `theme.ts` (theme read, apply, storage, system sync), `copy.ts` (clipboard and mailto helpers, toast copy strings), `utils.ts` (`cn()`).\n\n## Data Flow\n\n### Primary Request Path (page load)\n\n1. Browser loads `index.html` (or `404.html` for unknown paths on GitHub Pages) and the module script (`src/main.tsx`).\n2. `src/main.tsx` imports the font CSS and `src/styles/index.css`, then `createRoot(...).render(<App />)` in `StrictMode`.\n3. `src/app/App.tsx` renders `Providers`, which mounts `MotionPreferenceProvider`, then `MotionFeatures` (which creates `LazyMotion` with a dynamic-import loader from `src/anim/features.ts`), then `ToastProvider`.\n4. `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent` from `src/content/index.ts`, derives `email` from the `email` contact entry, and passes `profile`, `experience`, `projects`, `skills`, `education`, and `otherLinks` as props to each section.\n5. `useActiveSection(SECTION_IDS)` in `src/app/Page.tsx` tracks the visible section and returns `lockTo`, which both `SiteHeader` and `PillNav` use for navigation.\n\n### Theme Change Flow\n\n1. `src/components/ui/ThemeToggle.tsx` calls `applyTheme` (via `src/hooks/useTheme.ts`).\n2. `src/lib/theme.ts` sets `html[data-theme]` first, then writes localStorage key `theme`. If storage throws, it sets an in-memory `unsavedChoice` flag.\n3. `subscribeTheme` (MutationObserver on `data-theme`, `storage` events, and `prefers-color-scheme` change) keeps the attribute in sync with stored or system values.\n\n### Copy Email Flow\n\n1. `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` handles the click, prevents default, and calls `copyText` from `src/lib/copy.ts`.\n2. On success it shows `COPY_SUCCESS` through `useToast`; on failure it shows `COPY_FALLBACK` and calls `openMail`.\n\n### Motion Preference Flow\n\n1. `src/anim/useMotionPreference.ts` exposes `reduced`.\n2. `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` holds the features loader while reduced motion is on, so the animation bundle is not downloaded for those visitors. Turning reduced motion off releases the loader.\n3. A warm-up import runs on idle (`requestIdleCallback`, fallback `setTimeout` 1500 ms).\n\n**State Management:**\n- No global store. State is local `useState`/`useRef`, two React contexts (`MotionPreferenceProvider`, `ToastProvider`), and the DOM attribute `html[data-theme]`.\n\n## Key Abstractions\n\n**`siteContent` (content bundle):**\n- Purpose: Single typed object for all page data.\n- Examples: `src/content/index.ts`, `src/content/schema.ts`\n- Pattern: Data modules use `as const satisfies ...` against types in `src/content/schema.ts` (see `src/content/contacts.ts`).\n\n**`ImageAsset`:**\n- Purpose: Image metadata with AVIF and WebP sources at build time.\n- Examples: `src/content/images.ts` (`logo()` helper, `aboutPhoto`)\n- Pattern: vite-imagetools `?as=picture` import, normalized into `ImageAsset`.\n\n**Motion barrel (`@/anim`):**\n- Purpose: The only allowed animation import surface outside `src/anim`.\n- Examples: `src/anim/index.ts` exports `MotionFeatures`, `MotionPreferenceProvider`, `useMotionPreference`, `springs`, `durations`, `easeOut`, `ThemeThumb`, `ToastPresence`, `NavIndicator`, `PillMorph`.\n- Pattern: Facade. Callers never import `motion/react` directly.\n\n**Toast context:**\n- Purpose: App-wide transient messages.\n- Examples: `src/components/ui/Toast.tsx`, `src/components/ui/useToast.ts`\n- Pattern: Provider plus hook; `ToastPresence` from `@/anim` animates entry and exit.\n\n**Section (`src/layout/Section.tsx`):**\n- Purpose: Common section wrapper with anchor id and heading structure.\n- Pattern: Wrapper component used by every section in `src/sections/`.\n\n## Entry Points\n\n**Browser entry:**\n- Location: `src/main.tsx` (loaded by `index.html`)\n- Triggers: Page load.\n- Responsibilities: Mount React tree, load global CSS and font.\n\n**Fallback entry:**\n- Location: `404.html`\n- Triggers: GitHub Pages serves it for unknown paths.\n- Responsibilities: Second Rollup input declared in `vite.config.ts` (`build.rolldownOptions.input.notFound`), so it is emitted as a built asset.\n\n**Build config entry:**\n- Location: `vite.config.ts`\n- Triggers: `vite`, `vite build`, `vite preview`, Vitest (via `vitest.config.ts`).\n- Responsibilities: Base path from `VITE_BASE`, plugins, `@` alias to `src/`.\n\n**Verification entry:**\n- Location: `package.json` `scripts.verify`\n- Triggers: CI in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` and `.github/workflows/deploy.yml`.\n- Responsibilities: Typecheck, lint, format, unit tests, path/color/contrast checks, build, budget, phone and image checks.\n\n## Architectural Constraints\n\n- **Threading:** Single-threaded browser main thread. No Web Workers in the source tree.\n- **Global state:** Module-level state in `src/lib/theme.ts` (`unsavedChoice`), `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (features loaded flag). The DOM attribute `html[data-theme]` is shared state.\n- **Circular imports:** None detected in the explored files.\n- **Lazy boundary:** `src/anim/features.ts` is loaded only via dynamic `import('./features')` from `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`; do not turn it into a static import.\n- **Enforced import rules** (`.oxlintrc.json`, verified by `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts`):\n  - `motion/react`, `motion/**`, `@react-spring/**`, `framer-motion/**` may be imported only from `src/anim/` and `src/components/motion-primitives/`.\n  - Animation must be imported through the `@/anim` barrel, not deep paths.\n  - `src/content/**` may be imported only by `src/app/Page.tsx`.\n  - Files in `src/anim/` must not import `@/sections`, `@/layout`, or `@/content`.\n\n## Anti-Patterns\n\n### Deep-importing animation internals\n\n**What happens:** A component imports `@/anim/physics/ThemeThumb` or `@/components/motion-primitives/text-effect` directly.\n**Why it's wrong:** The lint rule in `.oxlintrc.json` flags it, and `tests/unit/boundaries.test.ts` pins that behavior.\n**Do this instead:** Import from the barrel, `import { ThemeThumb } from '@/anim'` (see `src/anim/index.ts`). For the vendored primitive, follow the existing exception in `src/sections/Hero/` and do not add new direct imports.\n\n### Importing content in a section\n\n**What happens:** A section such as `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx` imports `@/content` to read data.\n**Why it's wrong:** Breaks the rule that only `src/app/Page.tsx` reads `siteContent`, and it couples presentational sections to the data source.\n**Do this instead:** Add a prop to the section and pass the data from `src/app/Page.tsx`, as `Hero` receives `profile` and `email` today.\n\n### Animating from `motion/react` in app code\n\n**What happens:** Importing `motion` or `animate` from `motion/react` in `src/sections/` or `src/app/`.\n**Why it's wrong:** Bypasses the reduced-motion provider and the lazy `LazyMotion` boundary.\n**Do this instead:** Use `m` components and hooks exposed through `src/anim/index.ts`, or add a declarative wrapper to `src/anim/declarative/`.\n\n## Error Handling\n\n**Strategy:** Degrade silently. Storage, `matchMedia`, clipboard, and dynamic import failures are caught and fall back to a static or session-only behavior.\n\n**Patterns:**\n- `try/catch` around `localStorage` in `src/lib/theme.ts`; in-memory fallback flag `unsavedChoice`.\n- Clipboard failure in `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts` shows a toast and opens `mailto:`.\n- A failed features import in `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx` returns a never-settling promise so animations stay static and no unhandled rejection is raised.\n\n## Cross-Cutting Concerns\n\n**Logging:** None in application code.\n**Validation:** Compile-time, via `satisfies` against `src/content/schema.ts` types. Build-time gates in `scripts/` (for example `check-images.mjs`, `check-no-phone.mjs`).\n**Authentication:** Not applicable.\n**Accessibility:** `src/layout/SkipLink.tsx`, `prefers-reduced-motion` handling in `src/anim/`, axe checks in e2e (`axe-core` devDependency), and contrast gate `scripts/check-contrast.mjs`.\n\n---\n\n*Architecture analysis: 2026-11-19*\n"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":3885,"cache_read_input_tokens":57483,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":3885,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":8,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"global","speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":"toolu_01TQsrYKU4gwz4HKmE9ywYTJ","agent_id":"ab292f3acd27cf53f","session_id":"528fc7c7-ca76-4a8d-986b-657c219e4755","uuid":"77cbab13-7245-410d-8e1a-1247bbe753e3","timestamp":"2026-11-20T05:43:03.692Z","request_id":"req_011ChDeRbvZ4JZKkmcNQQxcx","subagent_type":"gsd-codebase-mapper","task_description":"Scan codebase tech and arch"}

`src/main.tsx`: browser entry, `createRoot` render.\n- `index.html`: HTML shell for the main entry.\n- `404.html`: GitHub Pages fallback entry.\n\n**Configuration:**\n- `vite.config.ts`: build, base path, alias, plugins, two inputs.\n- `tsconfig.json`: TypeScript.\n- `.oxlintrc.json`: lint and import boundaries.\n- `prettier.config.mjs`: formatting.\n- `package.json`: scripts and dependency versions.\n\n**Core Logic:**\n- `src/app/Page.tsx`: page composition and scroll-spy wiring.\n- `src/lib/theme.ts`: theme state and sync.\n- `src/anim/MotionFeatures.tsx`: lazy motion boundary.\n- `src/content/images.ts`: build-time image imports.\n\n**Testing:**\n- `tests/unit/`: Vitest.\n- `tests/e2e/`: Playwright.\n- `tests/fixtures/`: lint-boundary fixtures.\n- `tests/setup.ts`: Vitest setup.\n\n## Naming Conventions\n\n**Files:**\n- React components: PascalCase `.tsx`, for example `src/layout/SiteHeader.tsx`, `src/sections/Hero/Hero.tsx`.\n- Hooks: camelCase starting with `use`, for example `src/hooks/useActiveSection.ts`, `src/components/ui/useCopyEmail.ts`.\n- Non-component modules: camelCase, for example `src/lib/theme.ts`, `src/anim/tokens.ts`, `src/content/projects.ts`.\n- Hyphenated for nav and config-like modules: `src/layout/nav-items.ts`, `src/anim/featuresStatus.ts` (camelCase variant), `.oxlintrc.json`.\n- Test files: `*.test.ts` in `tests/unit/`, `*.spec.ts` in `tests/e2e/`.\n- Scripts: kebab-case `.mjs`, for example `scripts/check-path-case.mjs`.\n\n**Directories:**\n- Section and feature folders: PascalCase, for example `src/sections/Projects/`.\n- Non-component folders: lowercase, for example `src/lib/`, `src/anim/declarative/`, `src/components/ui/icons/`.\n- `motion-primitives` is the one kebab-case folder under `src/components/`.\n\n**Path alias:**\n- `@/` maps to `src/` (`vite.config.ts` `resolve.alias`). Use `@/...` for cross-folder imports.\n\n## Where to Add New Code\n\n**New section:**\n- Implementation: `src/sections/<Name>/<Name>.tsx`, wrapped in `src/layout/Section.tsx`.\n- Data: add a typed module in `src/content/`, export it from `src/content/index.ts`, and add it to `siteContent`.\n- Wiring: render it in `src/app/Page.tsx`, pass data as props, and add its id to `SECTION_IDS` in `src/layout/nav-items.ts` so the nav and scroll-spy pick it up.\n- Tests: `tests/e2e/<name>.spec.ts`, and `tests/unit/` if logic is pure.\n\n**New UI component (shared):**\n- Implementation: `src/components/ui/<Name>.tsx`.\n- Tests: `tests/unit/` for logic, `tests/e2e/` for behavior.\n\n**New animation:**\n- Implementation: `src/anim/declarative/<Name>.tsx` (declarative) or `src/anim/physics/<Name>.tsx` (react-spring).\n- Export it from `src/anim/index.ts`. Callers import from `@/anim` only.\n\n**New content data:**\n- Implementation: `src/content/<name>.ts` with a type from `src/content/schema.ts`, using `as const satisfies ...` like `src/content/contacts.ts`.\n- Export from `src/content/index.ts`.\n\n**New image:**\n- Source: `src/assets/images/`.\n- Import: add an imagetools query in `src/content/images.ts` (`?w=...&format=avif;webp&as=picture`).\n\n**Utilities:**\n- Pure helpers: `src/lib/<name>.ts`.\n- Stateful browser hooks: `src/hooks/<name>.ts`.\n\n**New static asset:**\n- `public/<file>`, referenced with `import.meta.env.BASE_URL` or a relative path so `VITE_BASE` works.
